     Chairman’s Statement
     主席報告



     MR. ZHANG YONG                                                               張勇先生
     Chairman                                                                     主席

     I hereby present our annual report for the year ended December 31,           本人欣然向各位股東提呈我們截至2024
     2024 to the Shareholders.                                                    年12月31日止年度的年報。


     OVERVIEW                                                                     概覽

     In 2024, China’s economy overall maintained a stable yet progressive         2024年，中國經濟運行總體穩中有進。
     momentum. Within the catering segment, local governments rolled              在餐飲板塊，各地政府出台多項刺激消
     out a series of policies aimed at stimulating consumption, proactively       費的政策，積極促進餐飲消費。餐飲業
     boosting food and beverage consumption. Catering industry peers also         同行亦積極創新，豐富餐飲業態，茶
     actively innovated and diversified their types of catering operation, with   飲、炒菜等類別蓬勃發展、增長迅速，
     categories such as tea beverages and stir-fried dishes developing and        為消費者提供更豐富多元的選擇，推動
     growing rapidly, providing consumers with richer and more diversified        餐飲業的整體復甦和高質量發展。海底

SYSTEM SALES GROWTH FOR HAIDILAO RESTAURANTS                                 海底撈餐廳的系統銷售額增長率

In 2024, Haidilao restaurants achieved a year-on-year system sales           2024年，海底撈餐廳系統銷售額同比增
growth of 3.0%.                                                              長3.0%。

The system sales for Haidilao restaurants represent the total sales          海底撈餐廳的系統銷售額指所有海底撈
generated by all Haidilao-branded restaurants, including both                品牌餐廳所產生的銷售總額，包括自營
self-operated and franchised restaurants. Although sales from                餐廳及加盟餐廳。儘管加盟餐廳的銷售
franchised restaurants are not directly recognized as part of the            額並未在綜合損益及其他全面收益表中
Company’s revenue in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and        直接確認為本公司收入的一部分，但其
other comprehensive income, they contribute to our revenues through          通過持續的特許權使用費為我們的收入
ongoing royalty fees. We consider system sales growth a valuable             作出貢獻。我們認為系統銷售額增長率
metric for investors, as it serves as a key driver of our revenue and        對投資者而言是一項有價值的指標，因
profit, while reflecting the overall performance of our business.            為它是我們收入及利潤的重要驅動因
                                                                             素，同時反映了我們業務的整體表現。

to be a substitute for the presentation of the Company’ s financial             非國際財務報告準則財務計量數據並非
results in accordance with IFRS. Rather, the Company believes that the          旨在取代本公司根據國際財務報告準則
presentation of such non-IFRS financial measures provides investors             呈列財務業績。相反，本公司相信該等
with additional information, and removes items that the Company does            非國際財務報告準則財務計量數據的呈
not believe are indicative of its core operations, to better compare past       列可為投資者提供額外資料，剔除本公
and current results. We believe that the presentation of core operating         司認為無法代表其核心營運的項目，可

enhance the comparability of our historical results of operations with          為，呈列核心經營利潤（非國際財務報告
the trends in their underlying results of operations, while we utilize this     準則計量）為進一步加強我們過往經營業
metric to assess the performance of our core operations. We consider            績與其相關經營業績趨勢的可比性提供
both quantitative and qualitative factors when assessing whether to             了額外資料，同時我們利用該指標來評

the understanding to our ongoing financial and business performance             重大或可能影響對我們持續財務和業務
or trends.                                                                      表現或趨勢的理解的項目的影響進行調

     CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES OF THE COMPANY                              本公司的企業管治常規

     The Group is committed to achieving high standards of corporate            本集團致力達致高水準的企業管治，務
     governance to safeguard the interests of the Shareholders and to           求保障股東權益及提高企業價值及責任
     enhance corporate value and sense of responsibility.                       感。

     The Company has applied the principles as set out in the Corporate         本公司已應用上市規則附錄C1所載企業
     Governance Code in Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules.                       管治守則的原則。

     corporate governance and two levels of corporate governance                及兩個層面的企業管治常規如下：
     practices, as follows:

     (a)    code provisions, which listed issuers are expected to comply with   (a)   守則條文，期望上市發行人遵守或
            or to give considered reasons for deviation; and                          就偏離行為提供審慎考慮的理由；
                                                                                      及

     (b)    recommended best practices for guidance only, which listed          (b)   建議最佳常規，僅屬指引，鼓勵上
            issuers are encouraged to comply with.                                    市發行人遵守。

     The Company’s corporate governance practices are based on the              本公司的企業管治常規乃以企業管治守
     principles and code provisions as set out in the Corporate Governance      則所載原則及守則條文為依據。
     Code.

     The Company regularly reviews its compliance with corporate                本公司定期審閱遵守企業管治守則的情

            7.     Mechanism for the Board to Obtain Independent Views               7.   董事會可獲得獨立觀點和意見
                   and Opinions                                                           的機制

                   The Company recognizes that independence of the Board                  本公司確信董事會具備獨立性
                   is a key element of good corporate governance. The Board               是良好企業管治的重要元素。
                   has established mechanisms to ensure independent views                 董事會已建立機制，以確保董
                   and input are available to the Board. A summary of these               事會能夠獲得獨立觀點和意
                   mechanisms is set out below:                                           見。機制的概要載列如下：

                   (1)     The Board ensures the appointment of at least three            (1)   董事會確保委任至少三
                           independent non-executive Directors and at least one                 名獨立非執行董事，並
                           third of its members being independent non-executive                 且至少有三分之一的成
                           Directors. Further, independent non-executive Directors              員為獨立非執行董事。
                           will be appointed to Board committees as required                    此外，獨立非執行董事
                           under the Listing Rules and as far as practicable to                 將根據上市規則的規定
                           ensure independent views are available.                              並在實際可行情況下獲
                                                                                                委任為董事委員會成
                                                                                                員，以確保有獨立觀點
                                                                                                和意見。

                   (2)     The Nomination Committee strictly adheres to the               (2)   提名委員會嚴格遵守有
                           director nomination policy of the Company with regard                關提名及委任獨立非執
                           to the nomination and appointment of independent                     行董事的提名政策，並
                           non-executive Directors, and is mandated to assess                   獲授權每年評估獨立非
                           annually the independence of independent non-                        執行董事的獨立性，以
                           executive Directors to ensure that they can continually              確保他們能夠持續地作
                           exercise independent judgement.                                      出獨立判斷。

           professional development of the Directors and senior                      管理層之培訓及持續專

           and compliance manual applicable to employees and                         於僱員及董事之行為守
           the Directors;                                                            則及合規手冊；

           governance policies and practices, and make                               業管治政策及常規，並

     E.     DIRECTORS’ RESPONSIBILITIES FOR FINANCIAL                              E.   董事進行財務申報的責任
            REPORTING

            The Directors acknowledge their responsibility for preparing the            董事確認彼等編製本公司截至2024

     P.     CORPORATE CULTURE                                                   P.   企業文化

            “ Kindness ” is not only our corporate culture, but also our             「與人為善」是我們的企業文化，也
            philosophy of treating employees. We call on restaurant managers         是我們對待員工的理念。我們號召
            to care for employees and settle down to establish emotional             店經理關懷員工，並致力於以此形
            bonds among employees, between employees and restaurants                 成員工之間、員工與門店之間及員
            and between employees and Haidilao, thereby increasing the               工與海底撈之間的情感紐帶，加強
            cohesiveness. “Changing your future with your own hands” is our          凝聚力。「雙手改變命運」是我們的
            core value. We have set up a fair and clear promotion blueprint          核心價值觀。我們為門店員工設置
            for restaurant employees and adopt the piece wages system to             了公平、清晰的晉升通道，並實行
            link their individual remuneration with workloads and working            「計件工資」制度，讓員工的個人薪
            quality directly, which have effectively mobilized the enthusiasm        酬與勞動數量、質量直接掛鈎，有
            of employees. Once promoted to the restaurant managers,                  效調動了員工的積極性。而員工一
            employees have opportunities to enjoy performance commission.            旦晉升為店經理，則有機會享有門
                                                                                     店業績提成。

      Reasons for Adopting the Contractual Arrangements                           訂立合約安排的原因

      Shanghai Kiwa is principally engaged in the operation of Haidilao            上海基瓦主要從事運營海底撈應用程序
      app, which is a social media platform for the Haidilao’ s members           （ 為 海 底 撈 會 員 的 社 交 媒 體 平 台 ）及 海
      and Haidilao related Wechat Mini Program. Functions of the Haidilao          底撈相關微信小程序。海底撈應用程序
      app and Wechat Mini Program mainly consist of: (1) ordering-related          及微信小程序的功能主要包括：(1)訂餐
      functions such as online ordering and queuing or delivery services;          相關功能，如網上訂餐及排號或外賣服
      (2) electronic mall for members to shop online or exchange their             務；(2)電子商城，可供會員進行網上購
      membership points for products; (3) organizing and managing member           物或將其會員積分兌換為產品；(3)組織
      activities; (4) other functions such as advertising.                         及管理會員活動；(4)其他功能，如廣告。

      Pursuant to the Administrative Measures of Foreign Investment               根據《外商投資准入特別管理措施（負面
      Admission (Negative List) 2021 Revision (外商投資准入特別管理措                        清單） （2024 年版）》及其他相關法規，
      施(負面清單)(2024年版)) and other relevant regulations, (i) foreign                (i)禁止外商投資網絡遊戲運營業務（「禁
      investments in the businesses of online games operation are                 止類業務」）；(ii)就外商投資增值電信業
      prohibited (the “Prohibited Businesses”); (ii) for foreign investments      務，如經營電子商城供會員將其會員積
      in value-added telecommunication business, such as the operation            分兌換為產品、允許用戶發佈文字、圖

Risks Relating to the Contractual Arrangements                             與合約安排有關的風險

There are certain risks that are associated with the Contractual           與合約安排有關的若干風險包括：
Arrangements, including:

•     The Contractual Arrangements may not be as effective in              •     合約安排於控制上海基瓦及享有其
      providing control over and entitlement to the economic interests           經濟利益方面可能不如直接擁有權
      in Shanghai Kiwa as direct ownership.                                      有效。

•     Potential conflicts of interest among WFOE and Shanghai Kiwa         •     外商獨資企業與上海基瓦之間可能
      may exist.                                                                 存在潛在利益衝突。

•     The Contractual Arrangements may be subject to scrutiny of the       •     合約安排可能會受中國稅務機關審
      PRC tax authorities and additional tax may be imposed.                     查並產生額外稅項。

•     The WFOE ’ s ability to acquire the entire equity interests in       •     外商獨資企業收購上海基瓦的全部
      Shanghai Kiwa may be subject to various limitations and                    股權的能力可能面臨多項限制及重
      substantial costs.                                                         大成本。

•     Uncertainties exist with respect to the interpretation and           •     關於外商投資法的詮釋及執行存在
      implementation of the Foreign Investment Law.                              不確定性。

Company dated November 1, 2019.                                            期為2019年11月1日的公告。

RESPONSIBILITIES OF DIRECTORS AND THOSE CHARGED                           董事及治理層就綜合財務報表須承擔的
WITH GOVERNANCE FOR THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL                            責任
STATEMENTS

The directors of the Company are responsible for the preparation of       貴公司董事負責根據國際會計準則委員
the consolidated financial statements that give a true and fair view in   會頒佈的國際財務報告準則會計準則及
accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards as issued by the IASB and       《香港公司條例》的披露要求編製真實公
the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance,         平的綜合財務報表，並對其認為為使綜
and for such internal control as the directors determine is necessary     合財務報表的編製不存在由於欺詐或錯
to enable the preparation of consolidated financial statements that are   誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述所需的內部控
free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error.           制負責。

In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the directors are     在編製綜合財務報表時，董事負責評
responsible for assessing the Group’s ability to continue as a going      估 貴集團持續經營的能力，並在適用
concern, disclosing, as applicable, matters related to going concern      情況下披露與持續經營有關的事項，以
and using the going concern basis of accounting unless the directors      及使用持續經營為會計基礎，除非董事
either intend to liquidate the Group or to cease operations, or have no   有意將 貴集團清盤或停止經營，或別
realistic alternative but to do so.                                       無其他實際的替代方案。

Those charged with governance are responsible for overseeing the          治理層負責監督      貴集團的財務報告過
Group’s financial reporting process.                                      程。

      Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the            吾等的目標是就綜合財務報表整體而言
      consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from material            是否不存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重
      misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor’s         大錯誤陳述取得合理憑證，並按照吾等
      report that includes our opinion solely to you, as a body, in accordance       協定的委聘條款僅向 閣下（作為整體）
      with our agreed terms of engagement, and for no other purpose. We              出具載有吾等意見的核數師報告，且報
      do not assume responsibility towards or accept liability to any other          告不可用作其他用途。吾等並不就本報
      person for the contents of this report. Reasonable assurance is a high         告之內容對任何其他人士負責或承擔任
      level of assurance, but is not a guarantee that an audit conducted in          何責任。合理憑證是高層次的保證，但
      accordance with HKSAs will always detect a material misstatement               不能保證按照香港核數準則進行的審核
      when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are            總能發現存在的重大錯誤陳述。錯誤陳
      considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could           述可以由欺詐或錯誤產生，且倘合理預
      reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of users            期彼等可能個別或共同影響使用者將該
      taken on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.                 等綜合財務報表作為基準而作出的經濟
                                                                                     決定，則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。

      As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise professional         在根據香港核數準則進行審核的過程
      judgment and maintain professional skepticism throughout the audit.            中，吾等運用了專業判斷，並保持專業
      We also:                                                                       懷疑態度。吾等亦：

             provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of not detecting a material       審核憑證，以為吾等的意見提供基
             misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for one resulting          礎。由於欺詐可能涉及串謀、偽
             from error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery, intentional            造、蓄意遺漏、虛假陳述，或淩駕

3.1 Basis of preparation of consolidated financial statements              3.1 綜合財務報表之編製基準

     The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in                綜合財務報表根據國際會計準則理
     accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards issued by the IASB.              事會頒佈的國際財務報告準則會計
     For the purpose of preparation of the consolidated financial               準則編製。就編製綜合財務報表而
     statements, information is considered material if such information         言，倘有關資料合理預期會影響主
     is reasonably expected to influence decisions made by primary              要使用者作出之決定，則有關資料
     users. In addition, the consolidated financial statements include          被視為重大。此外，綜合財務報表
     applicable disclosures required by the Rules Governing the Listing         包括香港聯合交易所有限公司證券
     of Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the              上市規則（「上市規則」）及《香港公
     “Listing Rules”) and by the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.                 司條例》所規定的適用披露。

     The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on the            綜合財務報表按歷史成本基準編
     historical cost basis except for certain financial instruments that        製，惟若干金融工具按各報告期末
     are measured at fair values at the end of each reporting period, as        的公允值計量，如下文所載的會計
     explained in the accounting policies set out below.                        政策所解釋。

     Basis of consolidation                                                     綜合基準

     The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial            綜合財務報表包括本公司及由本公
     statements of the Company and entities (including structured               司及其附屬公司所控制的實體（包
     entities) controlled by the Company and its subsidiaries. Control          括結構性實體）的財務報表。本公
     is achieved when the Company:                                              司在下列情況下取得控制權：

     •    has power over the investee;                                          •   具有對投資對象的權力；

     •    is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its               •   因參與投資對象業務而承擔可
          involvement with the investee; and                                        變回報的風險或享有可變回報
                                                                                    的權利；及

     •    has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.               •   能夠使用其權力以影響其回
                                                                                    報。

     The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if             倘事實及情況顯示上文所列控制權
     facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or          的三項元素的一項或多項有變，則
     more of the three elements of control listed above.                        本集團重新評估其是否控制投資對
                                                                                象。

             Leases                                                                    租賃

             The Group assesses whether a contract is or contains a lease              本集團於合約開始時根據國際財務
             based on the definition under IFRS 16 Leases at inception of the          報告準則第16號租賃 的定義評估合
             contract. Such contract will not be reassessed unless the terms           約是否為或包含租賃。該合約將不
             and conditions of the contract are subsequently changed.                  會重新進行評估，除非該合約中的
                                                                                       條款及條件隨後被改動。

             The Group as lessee                                                       本集團作為承租人

             Allocation of consideration to components of a contract                   將合約代價分配至各組成部分

             For a contract that contains a lease component and one or more            就包含一項租賃組成部分及一項或
             additional lease or non-lease components, the Group allocates             多項額外租賃或非租賃組成部分的
             the consideration in the contract to each lease component on the          合約而言，本集團根據租賃組成部
             basis of the relative stand-alone price of the lease component and        分的相對獨立價格及非租賃組成部
             the aggregate stand-alone price of the non-lease components.              分的獨立價格總和，將合約代價分
                                                                                       配至各租賃組成部分。

             Non-lease components are separated from lease component and               非租賃組成部分與租賃組成部分分
             are accounted for by applying other applicable standards.                 開，並應用其他適用準則入賬。

             have a lease term of 12 months or less from the commencement              賃，即自生效日期起計之租期為12
             date and do not contain a purchase option. Lease payments on              個月或以下並且不包括購買選擇權
             short-term leases are recognized as expense on a straight-line            的租賃。短期租賃的租賃付款於租
             basis over the lease term.                                                期內按直線法確認為開支。

             Lease liabilities                                                           租賃負債

             At the commencement date of a lease, the Group recognizes and               於租賃開始日期，本集團按該日並
             measures the lease liability at the present value of lease payments         未支付的租賃付款現值確認並計量
             that are unpaid at that date. In calculating the present value of           租賃負債。於計算租賃付款的現值
             lease payments, the Group uses the incremental borrowing rate               時，倘租賃內含利率無法確定，則
             at the lease commencement date if the interest rate implicit in the         本集團使用租賃開始之日的增量借
             lease is not readily determinable.                                          款利率計算。

             The lease payments include:                                                 租賃付款包括：

             •      fixed payments (including in-substance fixed payments) less          •   固 定 付 款（ 包 括 實 質 固 定 付
                    any lease incentives receivable;                                         款）減任何應收租賃獎勵；

             •      variable lease payments that depend on an index or a                 •   依賴於指數或利率的可變租賃
                    rate, initially measured using the index or rate as at the               付款，最初於開始日期使用指
                    commencement date;                                                       數或利率計量；

             •      amounts expected to be payable by the Group under                    •   本集團根據剩餘價值擔保預期
                    residual value guarantees;                                               應付的金額；

             •      the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group is              •   倘本集團合理確定行使選擇
                    reasonably certain to exercise the option; and                           權，則為購買選擇權的行使
                                                                                             價；及

             •      payments of penalties for terminating a lease, if the lease          •   倘租期反映本集團會行使選擇
                    term reflects the Group exercising an option to terminate the            權終止租賃，則計入終止租賃
                    lease.                                                                   的罰款。

             After the commencement date, lease liabilities are adjusted by              於開始日期之後，租賃負債根據利
             interest accretion and lease payments.                                      息增長及租賃付款進行調整。

             INFORMATION (Cont’d)

      3.2 Material accounting policy information (Cont’d)                            3.2 重大會計政策資料（續）

             Leases (Cont’d)                                                              租賃（續）

             The Group as lessee (Cont’d)                                                 本集團作為承租人（續）

             Lease modifications                                                          租賃修改

             The Group accounts for a lease modification as a separate lease              倘出現以下情況，本集團將租賃修
             if:                                                                          改作為一項單獨的租賃進行入賬：

             •      the modification increases the scope of the lease by adding           •   該項修改通過增加使用一項或
                    the right to use one or more underlying assets; and                       多項相關資產的權利擴大了租
                                                                                              賃範圍；及

             •      the consideration for the leases increases by an amount               •   增加租賃的代價，增加的金額
                    commensurate with the stand-alone price for the increase in               相當於範圍擴大對應的單獨價
                    scope and any appropriate adjustments to that stand-alone                 格，加上為反映特定合約的情
                    price to reflect the circumstances of the particular contract.            況而對單獨價格進行的任何適
                                                                                              當調整。

             For a lease modification that is not accounted for as a separate             就未作為一項單獨租賃入賬的租賃
             lease, the Group remeasures the lease liability, less any lease              修改而言，本集團基於透過使用修
             incentives receivable, based on the lease term of the modified               改生效日期的經修訂貼現率貼現經
             lease by discounting the revised lease payments using a revised              修訂租賃付款的經修訂租賃的租期
             discount rate at the effective date of the modification.                     重新計量租賃負債，減任何已收租
                                                                                          賃獎勵。

             The Group accounts for the remeasurement of lease liabilities by             本集團通過對有關使用權資產作出
             making corresponding adjustments to the relevant right-of-use                相應調整對重新計量租賃負債入賬。
             assets.

     Foreign currencies                                                           外幣

     In preparing the financial statements of each individual group               於編製各個別集團實體之財務報表
     entity, transactions in currencies other than the functional currency        時，以該實體功能貨幣以外之貨幣
     of that entity (foreign currencies) are recognized at the rates of           （外幣）進行之交易按交易日期之現
     exchanges prevailing on the dates of the transactions. At the end            行匯率確認。於報告期末，以外幣
     of the reporting period, monetary items denominated in foreign               計值之貨幣項目乃按當日之現行匯
     currencies are retranslated at the rates prevailing at that date.            率重新換算。以外幣計值按公允值
     Non-monetary items carried at fair value that are denominated in             列賬之非貨幣項目按釐定公允值當
     foreign currencies are retranslated at the rates prevailing on the           日之現行匯率重新換算。以外幣按
     date when the fair value was determined. Non-monetary items                  過往成本計量之非貨幣項目則毋須
     that are measured in terms of historical cost in a foreign currency          重新換算。
     are not retranslated.

     Exchange differences arising on the settlement of monetary items,            結算貨幣項目及重新換算貨幣項目
     and on the retranslation of monetary items, are recognized in                產生之匯兌差額於其產生期間在損
     profit or loss in the period in which they arise, except for exchange        益中確認，除未計劃或不大可能進
     differences on monetary items receivable from or payable to a                行結算的應收或應付予海外業務的
     foreign operation for which settlement is neither planned nor                貨幣項目的匯兌差額外（因此成為
     likely to occur (therefore forming part of the net investment in             海外業務投資淨額的一部分），其初
     the foreign operation), which are recognized initially in other              始於其他全面收益中確認且於出售
     comprehensive income and reclassified from equity to profit or               或部分出售本集團於附屬公司的權
     loss on disposal or partial disposal of the Group’s interests in             益時自權益重新分類至損益。
     subsidiaries.

     For the purpose of presenting the consolidated financial                     就呈列綜合財務報表而言，本集團
     statements, the assets and liabilities of the Group’s operations             業務的資產及負債乃使用於報告期
     are translated into the presentation currency of the Group (RMB)             末當前的匯率換算為本集團的呈列
     using exchange rates prevailing at the end of the reporting                  貨幣（即人民幣）。收入及開支項目
     period. Income and expenses items are translated at the average              按期間平均匯率換算，除非期間匯
     exchange rates for the period, unless exchange rates fluctuate               率劇烈波動，則使用交易日期的匯
     significantly during that period, in which case the exchange                 率換算。所產生的匯兌差額（如有）
     rates at the date of transactions are used. Exchange differences             乃於其他全面收益內確認，並於權
     arising, if any, are recognized in other comprehensive income and            益內的外匯儲備項下累計（屬於非
     accumulated in equity under the heading of translation reserve               控股權益（倘適用））。
     (attributed to non-controlling interests as appropriate).

             Taxation                                                                        稅項

             Income tax expense represents the sum of current and deferred                   所得稅開支指現時及遞延所得稅開
             income tax expense.                                                             支的總和。

             The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year.              現時應繳稅項乃按年度應課稅利潤
             Taxable profit differs from profit (loss) before tax because of                 計算。應課稅利潤因其他年度的應
             income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other years                 課稅或可扣稅的收益或費用及毋須
             and items that are never taxable or deductible. The Group ’ s                   課稅或不可扣稅的項目而有別於除
             liability for current tax is calculated using tax rates that have been          稅前利潤（虧損）。本集團的即期稅
             enacted or substantively enacted by the end of the reporting                    項負債乃按報告期末已頒佈或實質
             period.                                                                         上已頒佈的稅率計算。

             Deferred tax is recognized on temporary differences between                     遞延稅項就綜合財務報表內資產及
             the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the consolidated              負債賬面值與計算應課稅溢利所採
             financial statements and the corresponding tax bases used in the                用相應稅基的暫時性差異而確認。
             computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally           遞延稅項負債一般就所有應課稅暫
             recognized for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax                  時性差異確認。遞延稅項資產則一
             assets are generally recognized for all deductible temporary                    般在有可能出現應課稅溢利可用作
             differences to the extent that it is probable that taxable profits will         抵銷可扣減暫時性差異的情況下就
             be available against which those deductible temporary differences               所有可扣減暫時性差異作確認。倘
             can be utilized. Such deferred tax assets and liabilities are not               暫時性差異因一項不會影響應課稅
             recognized if the temporary difference arises from the initial                  溢利或會計溢利及於交易時不會產
             recognition (other than in a business combination) of assets and                生等額應課稅及可扣減暫時性差異
             liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor        的交易中的資產及負債獲初始確認
             the accounting profit and at the time of the transaction does not               （業務合併除外）而產生，則該等遞
             give rise to equal taxable and deductible temporary differences. In             延稅項資產及負債將不予確認。此
             addition, deferred tax liabilities are not recognized if the temporary          外，倘暫時性差異產生自商譽的初
             difference arises from the initial recognition of goodwill.                     始確認，則遞延稅項負債將不予確
                                                                                             認。

             Deferred tax liabilities are recognized for taxable temporary                   遞延稅項負債就於附屬公司的投資
             differences associated with investments in subsidiaries and                     及於聯營公司的權益以及於合營企
             interests in associates, and interests in joint ventures, except                業的權益有關的應課稅暫時性差異
             where the Group is able to control the reversal of the temporary                確認，除非本集團能夠控制暫時性
             difference and it is probable that the temporary difference will                差異的撥回及暫時性差異有可能於
             not reverse in the foreseeable future. Deferred tax assets arising              可預見將來不會撥回。有關該等投
             from deductible temporary differences associated with such                      資及權益的可扣減暫時性差異產生
             investments and interests are only recognized to the extent that                的遞延稅項資產僅於可能有足夠應
             it is probable that there will be sufficient taxable profits against            課稅利潤以動用暫時性差異的利益
             which to utilize the benefits of the temporary differences and they             時並預期於可預見將來撥回才予以
             are expected to reverse in the foreseeable future.                              確認。

3.2 Material accounting policy information (Cont’d)                        3.2 重大會計政策資料（續）

     Property, plant and equipment                                              物業、廠房及設備

     Property, plant and equipment are tangible assets that are held            物業、廠房及設備指持有用作生產
     for use in the production or supply of goods or services, or for           或提供貨物或服務或作行政用途的
     administrative purposes. Property, plant and equipment other than          有形資產。物業、廠房及設備（下
     freehold lands and renovation in progress as described below               文所述永久業權土地及進行中的裝
     are stated in the consolidated statement of financial position at          修除外）乃按成本減其後累計折舊
     cost less subsequent accumulated depreciation and subsequent               及其後累計減值虧損（如有）於綜合
     accumulated impairment losses, if any.                                     財務狀況表列賬。

     Freehold lands are not depreciated and are measured at cost less           永久業權土地不計折舊，並按成本
     subsequent accumulated impairment losses, if any.                          減其後累計減值虧損（如有）計量。

     Renovation in progress are carried at cost, less any recognized            進行中的裝修按成本減任何已確認
     impairment loss. Costs include any costs directly attributable             減值虧損列賬。成本包括將資產運
     to bringing the asset to the location and condition necessary              至所需地點及達到所需條件而能按
     for it to be capable of operating in the manner intended by the            管理層擬定的方式運作所直接產生
     management. Depreciation of these assets, on the same basis as             的任何成本。當資產可作擬定用途
     other property assets, commences when the assets are ready for             時，該等資產開始按與其他物業資
     their intended use.                                                        產相同之基準計算折舊。

     Depreciation is recognized so as to write off the cost of assets           確認折舊旨在按其估計可使用年期
     other than freehold lands and renovation in progress less their            內以直線法撇銷資產（永久業權土
     residual values over their estimated useful lives, using the               地及進行中的裝修除外）成本減剩
     straight-line method. The estimated useful lives, residual values          餘價值。估計可使用年期、剩餘價
     and depreciation method are reviewed at the end of the reporting           值及折舊方法於報告期末審閱，而
     period, with the effect of any changes in estimate accounted for           任何估計變動之影響按前瞻基準列
     on a prospective basis.                                                    賬。

     An item of property, plant and equipment is derecognized upon              物業、廠房及設備項目於出售或預
     disposal or when no future economic benefits are expected to               期不會因持續使用該資產產生未來
     arise from the continued use of the asset. Any gain or loss arising        經濟利益時終止確認。因出售或報
     on the disposal or retirement of an item of property, plant and            廢物業、廠房及設備項目所產生之
     equipment is determined as the difference between the sales                任何收益或虧損按資產之出售所得
     proceeds and the carrying amount of the asset and is recognized            款項與賬面值之差額釐定並於損益
     in profit or loss.                                                         中確認。

             effective interest rate, transaction costs and other premiums                 所支付或收取屬實際利率組成部分
             or discounts) through the expected life of the financial asset or             的所有費用及費率、交易成本及其
             financial liability, or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the          他溢價或折讓）準確貼現至初始確
             net carrying amount on initial recognition.                                   認時賬面淨值的利率。

             credit loss (“ECL”) model on financial assets (including rental                 第9 號金融工具 計提減值評估的金

             Instruments . The amount of ECL is updated at each reporting date               信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）模式進
             to reflect changes in credit risk since initial recognition.                    行減值評估。預期信貸虧損金額於

                                                                                             認以來信貸風險的變動。

             Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all possible               整個生命期的預期信貸虧損指於有
             default events over the expected life of the relevant financial                  關金融工具預期年期內所有可能發
             instrument. In contrast, 12-months ECL (“12m ECL”) represents                    生的違約事件產生的預期信貸虧
             the portion of lifetime ECL that is expected to result from default              損。相反，12個月的預期信貸虧損
             events that are possible within 12 months after the reporting date.             （「12 個月的預期信貸虧損」）指於
             Assessments are done based on the Group’s historical credit loss                 報告日期後12個月內可能發生的違
             experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors,               約事件預期將產生的信貸虧損，是
             general economic conditions and an assessment of both the                        整個生命期的預期信貸虧損的一部
             current conditions at the reporting date as well as the forecast of              分。評估乃根據本集團的歷史信貸
             future conditions.                                                               虧損經驗進行，並根據債務人特定

                                                                                              當前狀況及對未來狀況預測的評估
                                                                                              作出調整。

             The Group always recognizes lifetime ECL for trade receivables.                 本集團通常就貿易應收款項確認整

             characteristics.                                                                險特點相似的金融工具組合按綜合
                                                                                             基準進行評估。

             For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss allowance                就所有其他工具而言，本集團會計
             equal to 12m ECL, unless when there has been a significant                      量相等於12個月的預期信貸虧損的

             the Group recognizes lifetime ECL. The assessment of whether                    貸風險顯著增加，則在此情況下本

           at the reporting date with the risk of a default occurring on                約事件的風險與在初始確認日
           the financial instrument as at the date of initial recognition.              期金融工具發生違約事件的風
           In making this assessment, the Group considers both                          險。於作出有關評估時，本集
           quantitative and qualitative information that is reasonable                  團會考慮合理及可靠的定量及
           and supportable, including historical experience and                         定性資料，包括過往經驗及毋
           forward-looking information that is available without undue                  須付出過多成本或努力可取得
           cost or effort.                                                              的前瞻性資料。

           significantly since initial recognition when contractual                     過 30 天，則信貸風險自初始
           payments are more than 30 days past due, unless the                          確認以來顯著增加，除非本集
           Group has reasonable and supportable information that                        團有能說明信貸風險並無顯著
           demonstrates otherwise.                                                      增加的合理可靠資料，則作別
                                                                                        論。

           The Group regularly monitors the effectiveness of the criteria               本集團定期監控識別信貸風險
           used to identify whether there has been a significant increase               是否出現顯著增加所用標準的
           in credit risk and revises them as appropriate to ensure that                有效性，並對其作出適當修訂
           the criteria are capable of identifying significant increase in              以確保在款項逾期前有關標準

             (ii)    Definition of default                                                  (ii)    違約的定義

                     For internal credit risk management, the Group considers                       就內部信貸風險管理而言，本
                     an event of default occurs when information developed                          集團認為，倘內部生成或外部
                     internally or obtained from external sources indicates that                    來源獲得的資料顯示債務人不
                     the debtor is unlikely to pay its creditors, including the                     太可能向其債權人（包括本集
                     Group, in full (without taking into account any collaterals                    團）悉數付款（不考慮本集團
                     held by the Group).                                                            持有的任何抵押品），則發生
                                                                                                    違約事件。

                     Irrespective of the above, the Group considers that default                    無論上述情形如何，本集團認
                     has occurred when a financial asset is more than 90 days                       為，倘金融資產逾期超過 90
                     past due unless the Group has reasonable and supportable                       天，則發生違約事件，惟本集
                     information to demonstrate that a more lagging default                         團擁有合理有據資料證明更寬
                     criterion is more appropriate.                                                 鬆的違約標準更為合適除外。

             (iii)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                                     (iii)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認

                     The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability                        計量預期信貸虧損起到計算違
                     of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the                      約概率、違約損失率（即違約
                     loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default. The                   的虧損大小）及違約風險暴露
                     assessment of the probability of default and loss given                        的作用。評估違約概率及違約
                     default is based on historical data and forward-looking                        損失率乃基於歷史數據及前瞻
                     information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and                        性資料。估計預期信貸虧損反
                     probability-weighted amount that is determined with the                        映公正的概率加權金額，並以
                     respective risks of default occurring as the weights. The                      各自出現違約的風險為權重而
                     Group uses a practical expedient in estimating ECL on trade                    釐定。本集團經考慮歷史信貸
                     receivables using a provision matrix taking into consideration                 虧損經驗後使用撥備矩陣並採
                     historical credit loss experience, adjusted for forward looking                用可行權益方法估計貿易應收
                     information that is available without undue cost or effort.                    款項的預期信貸虧損，並按毋
                                                                                                    需花費不必要成本或精力可取

                                                                                  予以確認。

     cost using the effective interest method.                                    按攤餘成本計量。

     long term bonds, amounts due to related parties, trade payables,             款、長期債券、應付關聯方款項、

       revenue recognition policies

       The Group principally generates revenue from restaurant                      本集團的收入主要來自餐廳經營、

       Restaurant operation                                                         餐廳經營

       Revenue from restaurant operation is recognized when a customer              餐廳經營收入於顧客取得食物並於
       takes possession of the food and pays the bill in the restaurant,            餐廳支付賬單時（即已獲提供承諾
       which is when the promised services have been rendered.                      的服務時）確認。

       The Group operates a customer loyalty scheme through which                   本集團設立會員積分計劃，藉以向
       award credits are granted to the customers on consuming in the               於餐廳消費或下達外賣服務訂單的
       restaurants or ordering the delivery services, that entitle them             顧客授予積分獎勵，而顧客日後於
       to consume by offsetting the award credits on future purchases               餐廳購買及消費時可動用獎勵積分
       and consumptions in the restaurants. The promise to provide                  進行抵銷。因此，對顧客的權利承
       the right to the customer is therefore a separate performance                諾構成獨立履約責任。交易價格按
       obligation. The transaction price is allocated between the                   相對獨立的售價基準於所提供餐廳
       restaurant operation service or delivery service provided and the            經營服務或外賣服務與獎勵積分之
       award credits on a relative stand-alone selling price basis. The             間作出分配。各項獎勵積分的獨立
       stand-alone selling price of each award credit is estimated based            售價乃根據顧客兌換獎勵積分時所
       on the right to be given when the award credits are redeemed by              給予的權利及本集團過往經驗顯示
       the customer and the likelihood of redemption, as evidenced by               的積分兌換可能性進行估計。
       the Group’s historical experience.

       Award credits under the customer loyalty scheme are recognized               會員積分計劃項下的獎勵積分於自
       as contract liabilities when the transaction price for the initial           顧客收取初始餐廳服務的交易價格
       restaurant services received from the customers. Revenue from                時確認為合約負債。積分計劃所得
       the loyalty scheme is recognized when the award credits are                  收入於顧客兌換獎勵積分時予以確
       redeemed by the customer. Revenue for award credits that are                 認。預期不會進行兌換的獎勵積分
       not expected to be redeemed is recognized in proportion to the               之收入乃根據顧客行使權利的模式
       pattern of rights exercised by customers.                                    按比例確認。

       Proceeds received from the prepaid cards and vouchers issued                 自本集團發行的預付卡及禮券獲得
       by the Group, which can be utilized in the future consumption in             的收益，顧客可於日後在餐廳消費
       restaurants by the customers are recognized as contract liabilities          時使用，乃確認為合約負債，並於
       and recognized as revenue when utilized by the customers.                    客戶消費時確認為收入。

             Franchise business                                                            加盟業務

             to operate restaurants under the brand name of “Haidilao” and                 盟商」）以「海底撈」品牌經營餐廳
             provide management services. As franchisor, the Group is also                 並提供管理服務。作為特許權授予
             obliged to develop customary business practice to undertake                   者，本集團亦有義務制定開展餐廳
             restaurant operation such as analyzing consumers’ changing                    經營業務的慣常商業慣例，例如分
             preferences and implementing cuisine improvements, pricing                    析消費者不斷變化的喜好，並改良
             strategies, marketing campaigns and operational efficiencies to               菜餚、調整定價策略及營銷活動，
             support the franchise name. The Group concludes that these                    提高營運效率，以支持特許經營品
             activities are not distinct from the promise to grant franchise               牌。本集團認為，該等活動與承諾
             license.                                                                      授予特許經營許可證並無區別。

             The revenue from franchise business is recognized over time as                特許經營業務的收入是於一段時間
             the franchisees simultaneously receive and consume the benefits               內確認的，因為加盟商在接受服務
             provided by the Group ’ s franchise license and management                    的同時也在消耗集團特許經營許可
             services. The sales-based royalty fee is recognized as the                    和管理服務所提供的益處。基於銷
             franchisee’s subsequent sales occurs. The revenue relating to the             售額的特許權使用費在加盟商後續
             management services is recognized when the service is provided.               產生銷售時確認。與管理服務相關

    above tax losses and deductible temporary difference due to            未來溢利流且不大可能有應課稅溢
    the unpredictability of future profit streams of those loss-making     利可抵銷可動用稅項虧損，故並無
    subsidiaries and it is not probable that taxable profit will be        就上述稅項虧損及可扣減暫時性差
    available against which the tax losses can be utilized.                異確認遞延稅項資產。

     from operating activities as the main source of liquidity. For              活動產生的現金為主要流動資金

      41. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                 41. 資本風險管理

             The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group     本集團管理其資本旨在確保本集團
             will be able to continue as a going concern while maximizing the       內實體可持續經營，同時透過優化
             return to shareholders through the optimization of debt and equity     債務及權益平衡，盡量為股東帶來
             balances. The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged from          最大回報。本集團的整體策略自先
             prior year.                                                            前年度起保持不變。

             The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt, which         本集團的資本架構由負債淨額（包
             includes the bank borrowings disclosed in Note 31, other               括附註31所披露的銀行借款、其他
             borrowing, lease liabilities disclosed in Note 30, long term bonds     借款、附註30所披露的租賃負債、
             disclosed in Note 29, net of cash and cash equivalents and equity      附註29所披露的長期債券、扣除現
             attributable to owners of the Company, comprising issued share         金及現金等價物）及本公司擁有人
             capital, retained profits and other reserves.                          應佔權益（包括已發行股本、保留
                                                                                    盈利及其他儲備）組成。

             The management of the Group reviews the capital structure              本集團管理層定期覆核資本架構。
             regularly. As part of this review, the management of the Group         作為覆核的一部分，本集團管理層
             considers the cost of capital and the risks associated with each       考慮各類資本的成本及相關風險。
             class of capital. Based on recommendations of the management,          根據管理層的建議，本集團將透過
             the Group will balance its overall capital structure through the       支付股息、發行新股份及籌集、延
             payment of dividends, new shares issues as well as raising,            期及提前償還借款以平衡其整體資
             extension and early repayment of borrowings.                           本架構。
